def collect_predictions(
    model,
    loader,
):
    model.eval()

    diagnostic_probs = []
    diagnostic_true = []

    rhythm_probs = []
    rhythm_true = []

    total_loss = 0.0
    total_samples = 0

    for (
        signals,
        diagnostic,
        rhythm,
    ) in loader:

        signals = signals.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )

        diagnostic = diagnostic.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )

        rhythm = rhythm.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )

        outputs = model(
            signals
        )

        (
            loss,
            _,
            _,
        ) = compute_loss(
            outputs,
            diagnostic,
            rhythm,
        )

        batch_size = signals.size(0)

        total_loss += (
            loss.item()
            * batch_size
        )

        total_samples += (
            batch_size
        )

        diagnostic_probs.append(
            torch.sigmoid(
                outputs["diagnostic"]
            )
            .cpu()
            .numpy()
        )

        diagnostic_true.append(
            diagnos
CODE: def safe_macro_auroc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):
    values = []

    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        unique = np.unique(
            y_true[:, i]
        )

        if len(unique) < 2:
            continue

        values.append(
            roc_auc_score(
                y_true[:, i],
                y_prob[:, i],
            )
        )

    if not values:
        return float("nan")

    return float(
        np.mean(values)
    )


def safe_macro_auprc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):
    values = []

    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        unique = np.unique(
            y_true[:, i]
        )

        if len(unique) < 2:
            continue

        values.append(
            average_precision_score(
                y_true[:, i],
                y_prob[:, i],
            )
        )

    if not values:
        return float("nan")

    return float(
        np.mean(values)
    )
CODE: def find_optimal_thresholds(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    threshold_grid=None,
):
    if threshold_grid is None:

        threshold_grid = np.linspace(
            0.01,
            0.99,
            99,
        )

    thresholds = np.full(
        y_true.shape[1],
        0.5,
        dtype=np.float32,
    )

    threshold_f1 = np.zeros(
        y_true.shape[1],
        dtype=np.float32,
    )

    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        best_threshold = 0.5
        best_score = -1.0

        true = y_true[:, i]
        prob = y_prob[:, i]

        if len(
            np.unique(true)
        ) < 2:

            continue

        for threshold in (
            threshold_grid
        ):

            pred = (
                prob >= threshold
            ).astype(int)

            score = f1_score(
                true,
                pred,
                zero_division=0,
            )

            if score > best_score:

                best_score = score
                best_threshold = (
                    threshold
                )

        thresholds[i] = (
            best_threshold
        )

        threshold_f1[i] = (
            best_score
        )

    
CODE: def classification_metrics(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    thresholds,
):
    predictions = (
        y_prob >= thresholds
    ).astype(int)

    return {
        "auroc": safe_macro_auroc(
            y_true,
            y_prob,
        ),

        "auprc": safe_macro_auprc(
            y_true,
            y_prob,
        ),

        "f1": f1_score(
            y_true,
            predictions,
            average="macro",
            zero_division=0,
        ),

        "precision": precision_score(
            y_true,
            predictions,
            average="macro",
            zero_division=0,
        ),

        "recall": recall_score(
            y_true,
            predictions,
            average="macro",
            zero_division=0,
        ),
    }

    # "diagnostic_f1"
    # "diagnostic_auroc"
    # "combined_f1"
    # "combined_auroc"
    #
    # Start with combined_f1 because F1 is currently
    # the metric where your model has the largest room
    # for practical improvement.
    "metric": "combined_f1",

    "diagnostic_weight": 0.5,
    "rhythm_weight": 0.5,
}


def calculate_selection_score(
    metrics,
    config,
):
    metric_name = config[
        "metric"
    ]

    if metric_name == (
        "diagnostic_f1"
    ):

        return float(
            metrics[
                "diagnostic_f1"
            ]
        )

    if metric_name == (
        "diagnostic_auroc"
    ):

        return float(
            metrics[
                "diagnostic_auroc"
            ]
        )

    if metric_name == (
        "combined_f1"
    ):

        diagnostic_score = (
            metrics[
                "diagnostic_f1"
            ]
        )

        rhythm_score = (
            metrics.get(
                "rhythm_f1",
                diagnostic_score,
            )
        )

        total_weight = (
            config[
                "diagnostic_weight"
            ]

CODE: def per_label_report(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    thresholds,
    labels,
):

    rows = []

    for i, label in enumerate(
        labels
    ):

        true = y_true[:, i]
        prob = y_prob[:, i]

        pred = (
            prob >= thresholds[i]
        ).astype(int)

        if len(
            np.unique(true)
        ) < 2:

            auroc = np.nan
            auprc = np.nan

        else:

            auroc = roc_auc_score(
                true,
                prob,
            )

            auprc = average_precision_score(
                true,
                prob,
            )

        tn, fp, fn, tp = (
            confusion_matrix(
                true,
                pred,
                labels=[0, 1],
            )
            .ravel()
        )

        sensitivity = (
            tp / (tp + fn)
            if (tp + fn) > 0
            else np.nan
        )

        specificity = (
            tn / (tn + fp)
            if (tn + fp) > 0
            else np.nan
        )

        rows.append({
            "label": label,
            "AUROC": auroc,
            "AUPRC": auprc,
            "F1": f1_score(
                true,
                pred,

CODE: def plot_confusion_matrices(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    thresholds,
    labels,
    title_prefix,
):

    predictions = (
        y_prob >= thresholds
    ).astype(int)

    for i, label in enumerate(
        labels
    ):

        cm = confusion_matrix(
            y_true[:, i],
            predictions[:, i],
            labels=[0, 1],
        )

        plt.figure(
            figsize=(4, 4)
        )

        plt.imshow(
            cm,
            aspect="auto"
        )

        plt.xticks(
            [0, 1],
            ["Negative", "Positive"]
        )

        plt.yticks(
            [0, 1],
            ["Negative", "Positive"]
        )

        plt.xlabel(
            "Predicted"
        )

        plt.ylabel(
            "True"
        )

        plt.title(
            f"{title_prefix} — {label}"
        )

        plt.colorbar(
            label="Count"
        )

        for row_index in range(2):
            for col_index in range(2):

                plt.text(
                    col_index,
                    row_index,
                    str(
                        cm[
                            row_index,
                            col_index
               
CODE: def bootstrap_metric_ci(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    metric_fn,
    n_bootstrap=1000,
    seed=SEED,
):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(
        seed
    )

    n_samples = len(
        y_true
    )

    values = []

    for _ in range(
        n_bootstrap
    ):

        indices = rng.integers(
            0,
            n_samples,
            size=n_samples,
        )

        true_sample = (
            y_true[indices]
        )

        prob_sample = (
            y_prob[indices]
        )

        if len(
            np.unique(
                true_sample
            )
        ) < 2:

            continue

        values.append(
            metric_fn(
                true_sample,
                prob_sample,
            )
        )

    values = np.asarray(
        values
    )

    estimate = metric_fn(
        y_true,
        y_prob,
    )

    return {
        "estimate": float(
            estimate
        ),
        "lower_95": float(
            np.percentile(
                values,
                2.5
            )
        ),
        "upper_95": float(
            np.percentile(
                values,
                97.5
            )
        ),
    }
CODE: diagnostic_bootstrap_rows = []

for i, label in enumerate(
    diagnostic_targets
):

    true = (
        test_outputs[
            "diagnostic_true"
        ][:, i]
    )

    prob = (
        test_outputs[
            "diagnostic_probs"
        ][:, i]
    )

    if len(
        np.unique(true)
    ) < 2:

        continue

    ci = bootstrap_metric_ci(
        true,
        prob,
        roc_auc_score,
    )

    diagnostic_bootstrap_rows.append({
        "label": label,
        "AUROC": ci["estimate"],
        "CI_lower_95": ci["lower_95"],
        "CI_upper_95": ci["upper_95"],
    })

    diagnostic_bootstrap_rows
)

display(
    diagnostic_bootstrap_df

## 2. Define model, masked multi-label loss and epoch routines

The model outputs raw logits. Diagnostic and rhythm targets are independent multi-label outputs and use sigmoid/BCE, not softmax. The test partition is not used for early stopping or threshold selection.
CODE: MODEL_CONFIG = {
    "num_leads": 12,
    "num_diagnostic_classes": len(diagnostic_targets),
    "num_rhythm_classes": len(rhythm_targets),
    "cnn_channels": 96,
    "d_model": 128,
    "n_heads": 4,
    "n_layers": 3,
    "dropout": 0.20,
    "max_tokens": 250,
}
model = ECGTransformer(**MODEL_CONFIG).to(DEVICE)
criterion = MaskedBCEWithLogitsLoss()
task_weights = {"diagnostic": 1.0, "rhythm": 1.0}
if not rhythm_targets: task_weights.pop("rhythm")
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LEARNING_RATE, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(optimizer, mode="min", factor=0.5, patience=3, min_lr=1e-7)


def batch_targets(batch):
    targets = {}
    masks = {}
    for name in ["diagnostic", "rhythm"]:
        if name == "rhythm" and not rhythm_targets: continue
        value = batch["targets"][name].to(DEVICE).float()
        mask = torch.isfinite(value).float()
        targets[name] = torch.nan_to_num(value, nan=0.0)
        masks[name] = mask
    return targets, masks


def forward_logits(signal):
    output = model(signal)
    return {name: output[name] for name in task_weights}


def run_epoch(loader, training):
    model.
## 3. Train, select using validation loss and restore best weights
CODE: history = []
best_val_loss = float("inf"); best_state = None; best_epoch = 0; stale_epochs = 0
for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):
    train_loss = run_epoch(loaders["train"], training=True)
    val_loss = run_epoch(loaders["val"], training=False)
    scheduler.step(val_loss)
    history.append({"epoch": epoch, "train_loss": train_loss, "validation_loss": val_loss,
                    "learning_rate": float(optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"])})
    print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d}/{EPOCHS} | train={train_loss:.5f} | validation={val_loss:.5f}")
    if val_loss < best_val_loss - 1e-6:
        best_val_loss = val_loss; best_epoch = epoch; stale_epochs = 0
        best_state = {key: value.detach().cpu().clone() for key, value in model.state_dict().items()}
    else:
        stale_epochs += 1
        if stale_epochs >= PATIENCE:
            print(f"Early stopping; best epoch {best_epoch}.")
            break
if best_state is None: raise RuntimeError("No best validation checkpoint was produced.")
model.load_state_dict(best_state)
history_df = pd.DataFrame(history)
display(history_df.tail())

## 4. Export validation and test predictions, metrics and checkpoint

Validation predictions are retained for calibration. Test predictions are reserved for final evaluation only. Each row identifies a recording and has per-label probabilities; those probabilities represent PTB-XL annotation estimates, not a unified clinical disease probability.
CODE: def predict_dataframe(loader, split_name):
    model.eval(); rows = []
    with torch.no_grad():
        for batch in loader:
            signal = batch["signal"].to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True).float()
            output = model(signal)
            diagnostic_prob = torch.sigmoid(output["diagnostic"]).cpu().numpy()
            diagnostic_true = batch["targets"]["diagnostic"].cpu().numpy()
            rhythm_prob = torch.sigmoid(output["rhythm"]).cpu().numpy() if rhythm_targets else np.empty((len(signal), 0))
            rhythm_true = batch["targets"]["rhythm"].cpu().numpy() if rhythm_targets else np.empty((len(signal), 0))
            paths = batch.get("record_path", [None] * len(signal))
            for i in range(len(signal)):
                row = {"record_path": str(paths[i]), "split": split_name}
                for j, target in enumerate(diagnostic_targets):
                    row[f"{target}_true"] = float(diagnostic_true[i, j])
                    row[f"{target}_prob"] = float(diagnostic_prob[i, j])
                for j, target in enumerate(rhythm_targets):
                    row[f"{target}_true"] = float(rhythm_true[i, j])
                    row[f"{target}_prob"] = float

## 6. Comparison contract and scientific limitations

Compare this model against the original CNN-BiLSTM-Attention notebook using the same PTB-XL preprocessing version, patient-disjoint partitions, label definitions and metrics. Model selection should use validation results; the held-out test split must not be repeatedly used to tune architecture or thresholds.

This Transformer is an **ECG-only** model. It does not perform cross-modal attention over the biomarker and echocardiography branches. The six project datasets are unpaired, so no such patient-level attention can be trained from them.

CODE: SEED = 42


def seed_everything(seed=42):

    random.seed(seed)

    np.random.seed(seed)

    torch.manual_seed(seed)

    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)

    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False


seed_everything(SEED)

CODE: def calculate_pos_weight(
    dataframe,
    target_columns,
    device,
):

    weights = []

    for column in target_columns:

        positive = (
            dataframe[column]
            .sum()
        )

        negative = (
            len(dataframe)
            - positive
        )

        if positive <= 0:

            weight = 1.0

        else:

            weight = (
                negative
                / positive
            )

            weight = np.sqrt(
                weight
            )

        weights.append(
            weight
        )

    return torch.tensor(
        weights,
        dtype=torch.float32,
        device=device,
    )

CODE: DIAGNOSTIC_TASK_WEIGHT = 1.0
RHYTHM_TASK_WEIGHT = 1.0


def compute_loss(
    outputs,
    diagnostic_targets,
    rhythm_targets,
):

    diagnostic_loss = (
        diagnostic_criterion(
            outputs["diagnostic"],
            diagnostic_targets,
        )
    )


    rhythm_loss = (
        rhythm_criterion(
            outputs["rhythm"],
            rhythm_targets,
        )
    )


    total_loss = (

        DIAGNOSTIC_TASK_WEIGHT
        * diagnostic_loss

        +

        RHYTHM_TASK_WEIGHT
        * rhythm_loss
    )


    return (
        total_loss,
        diagnostic_loss,
        rhythm_loss,
    )

CODE: def augment_ecg(
    signals,
):

    if not AUGMENTATION_CONFIG[
        "enabled"
    ]:

        return signals


    batch_size = (
        signals.size(0)
    )

    num_leads = (
        signals.size(1)
    )

    signal_length = (
        signals.size(2)
    )


    out = signals


    # -----------------------------------
    # Amplitude scaling
    # -----------------------------------

    amplitude_config = (
        AUGMENTATION_CONFIG[
            "amplitude"
        ]
    )

    if amplitude_config[
        "enabled"
    ]:

        scale = (
            torch.empty(
                batch_size,
                1,
                1,
                device=signals.device,
                dtype=signals.dtype,
            )
            .uniform_(
                amplitude_config["low"],
                amplitude_config["high"],
            )
        )

        out = out * scale


    # -----------------------------------
    # Gaussian noise
    # -----------------------------------

    noise_config = (
        AUGMENTATION_CONFIG[
            "noise"
        ]
    )

    if noise_config[
        "enabled"
    ]:

        noise = (
            torch.randn_like(out)

CODE: def safe_macro_auroc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):

    scores = []

    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        unique_values = np.unique(
            y_true[:, i]
        )

        if len(unique_values) < 2:
            continue

        score = roc_auc_score(
            y_true[:, i],
            y_prob[:, i],
        )

        scores.append(
            score
        )

    if not scores:
        return float("nan")

    return float(
        np.mean(scores)
    )


def safe_macro_auprc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):

    scores = []

    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        unique_values = np.unique(
            y_true[:, i]
        )

        if len(unique_values) < 2:
            continue

        score = average_precision_score(
            y_true[:, i],
            y_prob[:, i],
        )

        scores.append(
            score
        )

    if not scores:
        return float("nan")

    return float(
        np.mean(scores)
    )
CODE: @torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(
    model,
    loader,
):

    model.eval()

    total_loss = 0.0
    total_samples = 0

    diagnostic_true = []
    diagnostic_probs = []

    rhythm_true = []
    rhythm_probs = []


    for batch in loader:

        signals = batch[
            "signal"
        ].to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )

        diagnostic_targets = (
            batch[
                "diagnostic"
            ].to(
                DEVICE,
                non_blocking=True,
            )
        )

        rhythm_targets = (
            batch[
                "rhythm"
            ].to(
                DEVICE,
                non_blocking=True,
            )
        )


        outputs = model(
            signals
        )


        (
            loss,
            _,
            _,
        ) = compute_loss(
            outputs,
            diagnostic_targets,
            rhythm_targets,
        )


        batch_size = (
            signals.size(0)
        )

        total_loss += (
            loss.item()
            * batch_size
        )

        total_samples += (
            batch_size
        )


        diagnostic_true.append(
      
CODE: def multilabel_metrics(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    thresholds=None,
):

    if thresholds is None:

        thresholds = np.full(
            y_true.shape[1],
            0.5,
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

    y_pred = (
        y_prob >= thresholds
    ).astype(int)


    return {

        "auroc":
            safe_macro_auroc(
                y_true,
                y_prob,
            ),

        "auprc":
            safe_macro_auprc(
                y_true,
                y_prob,
            ),

        "f1":
            f1_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
                average="macro",
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "precision":
            precision_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
                average="macro",
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "recall":
            recall_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
                average="macro",
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "micro_f1":
            f1_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
                average="micro",
                zero_division=0,

CODE: def find_optimal_thresholds(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):

    thresholds = []

    best_f1_values = []

    grid = np.linspace(
        0.05,
        0.95,
        91
    )


    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        true = y_true[:, i]
        prob = y_prob[:, i]


        if len(
            np.unique(true)
        ) < 2:

            thresholds.append(
                0.5
            )

            best_f1_values.append(
                np.nan
            )

            continue


        best_threshold = 0.5
        best_f1 = -1.0


        for threshold in grid:

            prediction = (
                prob >= threshold
            ).astype(int)


            score = f1_score(
                true,
                prediction,
                zero_division=0
            )


            if score > best_f1:

                best_f1 = score

                best_threshold = (
                    threshold
                )


        thresholds.append(
            best_threshold
        )

        best_f1_values.append(
            best_f1
        )


    return (
        np.asarray(
            thresholds,
            dtype=np.float32
        ),

        np.asa

CODE: def per_label_report(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    thresholds,
    labels,
):

    rows = []


    for i, label in enumerate(
        labels
    ):

        true = y_true[:, i]

        prob = y_prob[:, i]

        threshold = thresholds[i]

        prediction = (
            prob >= threshold
        ).astype(int)


        if len(
            np.unique(true)
        ) < 2:

            auroc = np.nan

            auprc = np.nan

        else:

            auroc = roc_auc_score(
                true,
                prob
            )

            auprc = (
                average_precision_score(
                    true,
                    prob
                )
            )


        tn, fp, fn, tp = (
            confusion_matrix(
                true,
                prediction,
                labels=[0, 1],
            )
            .ravel()
        )


        sensitivity = (
            tp / (tp + fn)
            if (tp + fn) > 0
            else np.nan
        )


        specificity = (
            tn / (tn + fp)
            if (tn + fp) > 0
            else np.nan
        )


        rows.append({

            "label":
                label,

            "AUROC":

CODE: import hashlib
def checkpoint_sha256(path):
    digest=hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path,'rb') as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda:stream.read(1024*1024),b''):digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()
model_sha256=checkpoint_sha256(checkpoint_path)
def long_predictions(output,frame,split):
    rows=[]
    for modality,names,true_values,prob_values in [
        ('diagnostic',diagnostic_targets,output['diagnostic_true'],output['diagnostic_probs']),
        ('rhythm',rhythm_targets,output['rhythm_true'],output['rhythm_probs'])]:
        for i,row in frame.reset_index(drop=True).iterrows():
            for j,name in enumerate(names):
                rows.append({'record_id':str(row.ecg_id),'patient_id':row.patient_id,'modality':modality,
                    'target':name,'y_true':int(true_values[i,j]),'y_probability':float(prob_values[i,j]),'split':split,'base_model_sha256':model_sha256})
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)

CODE: class EchoNetDataset(Dataset):

    def __init__(
        self,
        dataframe,
        project_root,
    ):
        self.df = dataframe.reset_index(
            drop=True
        )

        self.project_root = Path(
            project_root
        )

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.df)

    def _load_clip(
        self,
        video_path,
        frame_indices,
    ):
        video_path = (
            self.project_root
            / video_path
        )

        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(
            str(video_path)
        )

        if not cap.isOpened():
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Could not open video: "
                f"{video_path}"
            )

        frames = []

        for frame_index in frame_indices:

            cap.set(
                cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,
                int(frame_index)
            )

            ret, frame = cap.read()

            if not ret:
                cap.release()

                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Could not read frame "
                    f"{frame_index} from "
                    f"{video_path}"
                )

            # BGR -> grayscale
            gray = c

CODE: criterion = nn.MSELoss()

CODE: USE_AMP = torch.cuda.is_available()

scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler(
    "cuda",
    enabled=USE_AMP
)

print("Mixed precision enabled:", USE_AMP)
OUTPUT: Mixed precision enabled: True
CODE: def train_one_epoch(
    model,
    loader,
    criterion,
    optimizer,
    scaler,
):
    model.train()

    running_loss = 0.0
    samples = 0

    for clips, targets in loader:

        clips = clips.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True
        )

        targets = targets.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True
        )

        optimizer.zero_grad(
            set_to_none=True
        )

        with torch.autocast(
            device_type=DEVICE.type,
            dtype=torch.float16,
            enabled=USE_AMP,
        ):

            outputs = model(
                clips
            ).squeeze(1)

            loss = criterion(
                outputs,
                targets
            )

        scaler.scale(
            loss
        ).backward()

        scaler.unscale_(
            optimizer
        )

        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(
            model.parameters(),
            max_norm=1.0
        )

        scaler.step(
            optimizer
        )

        scaler.update()

        batch_size = clips.size(0)

        running_loss += (
            loss.item()
            * batch_size
        )

        samples += batch_size


CODE: @torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(
    model,
    loader,
    criterion,
):
    model.eval()

    total_squared_error = 0.0
    total_absolute_error = 0.0
    total_samples = 0

    all_predictions = []
    all_targets = []

    for clips, targets in loader:

        clips = clips.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True
        )

        targets = targets.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True
        )

        outputs = model(
            clips
        ).squeeze(1)

        squared_error = (
            (outputs - targets) ** 2
        )

        absolute_error = (
            torch.abs(
                outputs - targets
            )
        )

        batch_size = clips.size(0)

        total_squared_error += (
            squared_error.sum().item()
        )

        total_absolute_error += (
            absolute_error.sum().item()
        )

        total_samples += batch_size

        all_predictions.extend(
            outputs.cpu().numpy().tolist()
        )

        all_targets.extend(
            targets.cpu().numpy().tolist()
        )

    mse = (
        total_squared_error
        / total_samples
    )

    mae = (
        total_absolute_err

CODE: class EchoNetMaskedMSELoss(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, variances):
        super().__init__()
        self.variances = variances

    def forward(self, outputs, batch):
        total = None
        parts = {}
        target_columns = {"EF": "ef", "LVEDV": "edv", "LVESV": "esv"}
        for head, variance in self.variances.items():
            predicted = outputs[head].float().reshape(-1)
            target = batch[target_columns[head]].float().to(predicted.device).reshape(-1)
            mask = torch.isfinite(target)
            if not mask.any():
                continue
            mse = torch.mean((predicted[mask] - target[mask]) ** 2)
            parts[f"mse_{head}"] = mse.detach()
            total = mse / variance if total is None else total + mse / variance
        if total is None:
            raise ValueError("Batch has no finite EchoNet targets.")
        return total, parts

# No pretrained=False or Imagenet-only scratch initialization is used.
head_params = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if not n.startswith("encoder.")]
backbone_params = [p for n, p in model.named_parameters() if n.startswith("encoder.") and not n.endswith("time_encoder.pe")]

optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
    [
        {"params": head_params, "lr": 1e-4},
        {"params": backbone_params, "lr": 1e-5},
    ],
    weight_decay=1e-4,
)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(
    optimizer, mode="min", factor=0.5, patience=3
)

trainer = Trainer(
    model=model,
    optimizer=optimizer,
    loss_fn=EchoNetMaskedMSELoss(variances),

### ml-service\notebooks\03_training\echo\03_panecho_finetuning_training.ipynb

CODE: # Loss: calculate MSE for each available target
TARGET_KEYS = {"EF": "ef", "LVEDV": "edv", "LVESV": "esv"}

def calculate_loss(outputs, batch):
    losses = []
    details = {}

    for head, variance in variances.items():
        prediction = outputs[head].float().reshape(-1)
        target = batch[TARGET_KEYS[head]].to(DEVICE).float().reshape(-1)
        valid = torch.isfinite(target)

        if valid.any():
            mse = torch.mean((prediction[valid] - target[valid]) ** 2)
            losses.append(mse / variance)
            details[head] = float(mse.detach().cpu())

    if not losses:
        raise ValueError("No valid labels found in this batch.")

    return torch.stack(losses).sum(), details

                   if not name.startswith("encoder.") and p.requires_grad]
backbone_parameters = [p for name, p in model.named_parameters()
                       if name.startswith("encoder.") and p.requires_grad]

optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
    [
        {"params": head_parameters, "lr": 5e-5},
        {"params": backbone_parameters, "lr": 5e-6},
    ],
    weight_decay=1e-4,
)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.ReduceLROnPlateau(
    optimizer, mode="min", factor=0.5, patience=2
)
scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=USE_AMP)

print("Loaded pretrained heads:", model.task_names)
OUTPUT: Loaded pretrained heads: ['EF', 'LVEDV', 'LVESV']
OUTPUT: C:\Users\HP OMEN\AppData\Local\Temp\ipykernel_284872\3738978434.py:23: FutureWarning: `torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(args...)` is deprecated. Please use `torch.amp.GradScaler('cuda', args...)` instead.
  scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=USE_AMP)
CODE: # Run one training or validation epoch
def run_epoch(loader, training):
    model.train(training)
    total_loss = 0.0
    batch_count = 0

    for batch in loader:
        videos = batch["video"].to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)

        if training:
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)

        amp_context = (
            torch.autocast(device_type="cuda", dtype=torch.float16)
            if USE_AMP else nullcontext()
        )

        with torch.set_grad_enabled(training):
            with amp_context:
                outputs = model(videos)
                loss, _ = calculate_loss(outputs, batch)

            if training:
                scaler.scale(loss).backward()
                scaler.unscale_(optimizer)
                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()

        total_loss += float(loss.detach().cpu())
        batch_count += 1

    return total_loss / max(batch_count, 1)
CODE: # Fine-tune and keep the checkpoint with the best validation loss
history = []
best_val_loss = float("inf")
epochs_without_improvement = 0

for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):
    train_loss = run_epoch(loaders["train"], training=True)
    val_loss = run_epoch(loaders["val"], training=False)
    scheduler.step(val_loss)

    history.append({"epoch": epoch, "train_loss": train_loss, "val_loss": val_loss})
    print(f"Epoch {epoch:02d} | train loss: {train_loss:.4f} | val loss: {val_loss:.4f}")

    if val_loss < best_val_loss:
        best_val_loss = val_loss
        epochs_without_improvement = 0
        torch.save(
            {
                "model_state_dict": {k: v.detach().cpu() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()},
                "panecho_tasks": targets,
                "clip_len": 16,
                "epoch": epoch,
                "val_loss": val_loss,
                "source": "PanEcho fine-tuned on EchoNet-Dynamic",
            },
            OUTPUT_CHECKPOINT,
        )
        print("  Saved best model.")
    else:
        epochs_without_improvement += 1

    if epochs_without_improvement >= PATIENCE:
        print("Early stopping: validation loss did not improve.
OUTPUT: Epoch 01 | train loss: 1.3677 | val loss: 1.6108

def file_sha256(path):
    digest=hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path,'rb') as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda:stream.read(1024*1024),b''):digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

    optimizer, mode="min", factor=0.5, patience=2
)

  scaler = torch.cuda.amp.GradScaler(enabled=USE_AMP)
CODE: # Run one training or validation epoch
def run_epoch(loader, training):
    model.train(training)
    total_loss = 0.0
    batch_count = 0

    for batch in loader:
        videos = batch["video"].to(DEVICE, non_blocking=True)

        if training:
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)

        amp_context = (
            torch.autocast(device_type="cuda", dtype=torch.float16)
            if USE_AMP else nullcontext()
        )

        with torch.set_grad_enabled(training):
            with amp_context:
                outputs = model(videos)
                loss, _ = calculate_loss(outputs, batch)

            if training:
                scaler.scale(loss).backward()
                scaler.unscale_(optimizer)
                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()

        total_loss += float(loss.detach().cpu())
        batch_count += 1

    return total_loss / max(batch_count, 1)
CODE: history=[];best_val_loss=float('inf');epochs_without_improvement=0
for epoch in range(1,EPOCHS+1):
    train_loss=run_epoch(loaders['train'],training=True)
    val_loss=run_epoch(loaders['val'],training=False);scheduler.step(val_loss)
    history.append({'epoch':epoch,'train_loss':train_loss,'internal_holdout_loss':val_loss})
    print(f'Epoch {epoch:02d} | development {train_loss:.4f} | internal holdout {val_loss:.4f}')
    if val_loss<best_val_loss:
        best_val_loss=val_loss;epochs_without_improvement=0
        torch.save({'model_state_dict':{k:v.detach().cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()},
          'panecho_tasks':targets,'clip_len':16,'epoch':epoch,'val_loss':val_loss,
          'source':'PanEcho fine-tuned; internal training-fold holdout selection'},OUTPUT_CHECKPOINT)
    else:epochs_without_improvement+=1
    if epochs_without_improvement>=PATIENCE:break
(ARTIFACT_DIR/'history.json').write_text(json.dumps(history,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')

CODE: from torch.utils.data import Dataset


class BiomarkerTestDataset(Dataset):

    def __init__(
        self,
        X,
        y,
    ):
        self.X = torch.tensor(
            X,
            dtype=torch.float32
        )

        self.y = y.reset_index(
            drop=True
        )

        self.targets = list(
            self.y.columns
        )

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.X)

    def __getitem__(self, index):

        x = self.X[index]

        targets = {}

        for target in self.targets:

            value = self.y.iloc[
                index
            ][target]

            if pd.isna(value):

                if target in MULTICLASS_TARGETS:
                    value = -1
                    dtype = torch.long
                else:
                    value = float("nan")
                    dtype = torch.float32

            elif target in MULTICLASS_TARGETS:

                value = int(value)
                dtype = torch.long

            else:

                value = float(value)
                dtype = torch.float32

            targets[target] = torch.tensor(
                value,
                dtype=dtype
            )

CODE: validation_predictions = {}

for dataset_name, loader in val_loaders.items():

thresholds = {}

for dataset_name, predictions in validation_predictions.items():

    for target, values in predictions.items():

        if target in MULTICLASS_TARGETS:
            continue

        y_true = values["true"].astype(int)
        y_prob = values["pred"].astype(float)

        candidate_thresholds = np.unique(
            np.concatenate([
                np.array([0.0, 0.5, 1.0]),
                y_prob,
            ])
        )

        scores = [
            f1_score(
                y_true,
                (y_prob >= threshold).astype(int),
                zero_division=0,
            )
            for threshold in candidate_thresholds
        ]

        best_index = int(np.argmax(scores))
        thresholds[target] = float(
            candidate_thresholds[best_index]
        )

print("Validation-selected thresholds:")
for target, threshold in sorted(thresholds.items()):

CODE: from sklearn.metrics import confusion_matrix
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt


def plot_binary_confusion_matrix(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    title,
    threshold=0.5,
):
    y_pred = (
        y_prob >= threshold
    ).astype(int)

    cm = confusion_matrix(
        y_true,
        y_pred
    )

    plt.figure(figsize=(5, 4))

    plt.imshow(cm)

    plt.xticks(
        [0, 1],
        ["Negative", "Positive"]
    )

    plt.yticks(
        [0, 1],
        ["Negative", "Positive"]
    )

    plt.xlabel("Predicted")
    plt.ylabel("Actual")
    plt.title(title)

    for i in range(2):
        for j in range(2):
            plt.text(
                j,
                i,
                cm[i, j],
                ha="center",
                va="center"
            )

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.show()


zheen_predictions = (

CODE: def prediction_frame(prediction_map):
    rows = []

    for dataset_name, predictions in prediction_map.items():
        for target, values in predictions.items():
            for true, pred in zip(values["true"], values["pred"]):
                rows.append({
                    "dataset": dataset_name,
                    "target": target,
                    "true": true,
                    "prediction": pred,
                })

    return pd.DataFrame(rows)

    validation_predictions

# 2. DATASET LOADERS & CLINICAL BIOMARKER FEATURE ENGINEERING
# ==============================================================================

def _normalize_cols(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    df = df.copy()
    df.columns = [c.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_").replace("-", "_") for c in df.columns]
    return df


def load_and_engineer_zheen(path: str):
    """
    Zheen Hospital Heart Attack Dataset (1,319 records).
    Core Biomarkers: Troponin, CK-MB, Blood Sugar + Hemodynamics.
    """
    df = _normalize_cols(pd.read_csv(path))

    target_col = "result" if "result" in df.columns else df.columns[-1]
    if df[target_col].dtype == object:
        y = df[target_col].astype(str).str.strip().str.lower().map({"positive": 1, "negative": 0, "1": 1, "0": 0})
    else:
        y = df[target_col].astype(int)

    X = df.drop(columns=[target_col]).copy()

    hr_col = next((c for c in X.columns if "heart" in c or c == "impluse"), None)
    sbp_col = next((c for c in X.columns if "systolic" in c or "pressurehight" in c), None)
    dbp_col = next((c for c in X.columns if "diastolic" in c or "pressur
CODE: # ==============================================================================
# 4. ROC / PR CURVES & SHAP BIOMARKER EXPLAINABILITY
# ==============================================================================

def plot_roc_pr_curves(dataset_name: str, y: pd.Series, oof_probas: dict):
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(13, 5))

    for model_name, proba in oof_probas.items():
        fpr, tpr, _ = roc_curve(y, proba)
        auc_val = roc_auc_score(y, proba)
        axes[0].plot(fpr, tpr, label=f"{model_name} (AUC={auc_val:.3f})", lw=2)

        prec, rec, _ = precision_recall_curve(y, proba)
        ap_val = average_precision_score(y, proba)
        axes[1].plot(rec, prec, label=f"{model_name} (AP={ap_val:.3f})", lw=2)

    axes[0].plot([0, 1], [0, 1], "k--", alpha=0.5)
    axes[0].set_title(f"{dataset_name} — ROC Curve")
    axes[0].set_xlabel("False Positive Rate")
    axes[0].set_ylabel("True Positive Rate (Recall)")
    axes[0].legend(loc="lower right", fontsize=8)

    axes[1].set_title(f"{dataset_name} — Precision-Recall Curve")
    axes[1].set_xlabel("Recall")
    axes[1].set_ylabel("Precision")
    axes[1].legend(loc="lower left", fontsize=8)

    plt.tight_l

def find_root(start=None):
    current = Path(start or Path.cwd()).resolve()
    for p in [current, *current.parents]:
        if (p / "src").is_dir() and (p / "config").is_dir(): return p
        if (p / "ml-service" / "src").is_dir(): return p / "ml-service"
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not find ml-service.")

CODE: frames = []
for source in SOURCES:
    path = ARTIFACT_ROOT / source / "test_predictions.csv"
    if not path.is_file():
        print(f"[MISSING] {path} — run the FT-Transformer training notebook first.")
        continue
    frame = pd.read_csv(path)
    required = {"dataset", "target", "split", "row_index", "true", "prediction", "output_type"}
    missing = sorted(required - set(frame.columns))
    if missing: raise ValueError(f"{path} misses columns: {missing}")
    if set(frame["split"].dropna().unique()) != {"test"}:
        raise ValueError(f"{path} contains non-test rows; refusing to report it as held-out test evaluation.")
    frames.append(frame)
if not frames: raise FileNotFoundError("No FT-Transformer test prediction files found.")
predictions = pd.concat(frames, ignore_index=True)

## 2. Calculate per-source, per-target test metrics

Binary probabilities and multiclass distributions are reported separately. Metrics are not aggregated across the four different clinical populations or endpoint meanings.
CODE: def evaluate_binary(group):
    y = pd.to_numeric(group["true"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
    p = pd.to_numeric(group["prediction"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
    valid = np.isfinite(y) & np.isfinite(p); y, p = y[valid].astype(int), p[valid]
    if len(y) == 0 or len(np.unique(y)) < 2:
        return {"status": "not_computable_single_class_or_no_rows", "n": int(len(y))}
    return {"status": "computed", "n": int(len(y)), "auroc": float(roc_auc_score(y,p)),
            "auprc": float(average_precision_score(y,p)), "brier": float(brier_score_loss(y,p)),
            "f1_at_0_5": float(f1_score(y,p>=0.5,zero_division=0))}

CODE: def resolve_processed_path(value):
    path = Path(str(value))

    candidates = []

    if path.is_absolute():
        candidates.append(path)

    candidates.extend([
        PROJECT_ROOT / path,
        path,
        PROCESSED_DIR / path,
        PROJECT_ROOT / "data" / path,
    ])

    for candidate in candidates:
        if candidate.is_file():
            return candidate.resolve()

    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"Processed ECG signal was not found: {value}"
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        dataframe,
        project_root,
        diagnostic_targets,
    ):
        self.df = (
            dataframe
            .reset_index(drop=True)
        )

        self.project_root = Path(
            project_root
        )

        self.diagnostic_targets = list(
            diagnostic_targets

CODE: @torch.no_grad()
def collect_test_predictions(
    model,
    loader,
):
    model.eval()

    all_true = []
    all_prob = []

    for signals, diagnostic in loader:
        signals = signals.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )

        outputs = model(signals)

        probabilities = torch.sigmoid(
            outputs["diagnostic"]
        )

        all_true.append(
            diagnostic.numpy()
        )

        all_prob.append(
            probabilities
            .cpu()
            .numpy()
        )

    return (
        np.concatenate(
            all_true,
            axis=0,
        ),
        np.concatenate(
            all_prob,
            axis=0,
        ),
    )


diagnostic_true, diagnostic_prob = (
    collect_test_predictions(
        model,
        test_loader,
    )
)

print(
    "Diagnostic true shape:",
    diagnostic_true.shape,

print(
    "Diagnostic probability shape:",
    diagnostic_prob.shape,

CODE: def safe_auc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):
    if len(np.unique(y_true)) < 2:
        return np.nan

    return roc_auc_score(
        y_true,
        y_prob,
    )


def safe_auprc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):
    if len(np.unique(y_true)) < 2:
        return np.nan

    return average_precision_score(
        y_true,
        y_prob,
    )


diagnostic_pred = (
    diagnostic_prob
    >= diagnostic_thresholds
).astype(int)

    DIAGNOSTIC_COLUMNS
):
    true = diagnostic_true[:, i]
    prob = diagnostic_prob[:, i]
    pred = diagnostic_pred[:, i]

    cm = confusion_matrix(
        true,
        pred,
        labels=[0, 1],
    )

    tn, fp, fn, tp = cm.ravel()

    sensitivity = (
        tp / (tp + fn)
        if (tp + fn) > 0
        else np.nan
    )

    specificity = (
        tn / (tn + fp)
        if (tn + fp) > 0
        else np.nan
    )

    rows.append({
        "label": label,
        "AUROC": safe_auc(true, prob),
        "AUPRC": safe_auprc(true, prob),

    "Checkpoint exists":
        CHECKPOINT_PATH.exists(),

    "Test set non-empty":
        len(test_dataset) > 0,

    "Five diagnostic classes":
        len(DIAGNOSTIC_COLUMNS) == 5,

        len(diagnostic_true)
        == len(test_dataset),

    "Prediction dimension correct":
        diagnostic_prob.shape[1] == 5,

    "Metrics saved":
        summary_path.exists(),
}

    print(
        f"[{'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL'}] "
        f"{name}"
    )

if not all(checks.values()):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "1D CNN evaluation integrity check failed."
    )

print("\nAll integrity checks passed.")

CODE: SEED = 42


def seed_everything(seed=42):

    random.seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)

    if torch.cuda.is_available():

        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)


    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False


seed_everything(SEED)

CODE: state_dict = checkpoint[

clean_state_dict = {}


for key, value in state_dict.items():

    new_key = key

    if new_key.startswith(
        "module."
    ):

        new_key = new_key[
            len("module.") :
        ]


    clean_state_dict[
        new_key
    ] = value


missing_keys, unexpected_keys = (

CODE: @torch.no_grad()
def collect_predictions(
    model,
    loader,
):

    model.eval()


    diagnostic_true = []
    diagnostic_probs = []

    rhythm_true = []
    rhythm_probs = []


    for batch in loader:

        signals = batch[
            "signal"
        ].to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )


        diagnostic_targets = (
            batch[
                "diagnostic"
            ]
            .to(
                DEVICE,
                non_blocking=True,
            )
        )


        rhythm_targets = (
            batch[
                "rhythm"
            ]
            .to(
                DEVICE,
                non_blocking=True,
            )
        )


        outputs = model(
            signals
        )


        diagnostic_true.append(
            diagnostic_targets
            .cpu()
            .numpy()
        )


        diagnostic_probs.append(
            torch.sigmoid(
                outputs[
                    "diagnostic"
                ]
            )
            .cpu()
            .numpy()
        )


        rhythm_true.append(
            rhythm_targets
            .cpu()
            .numpy()
        )


   
OUTPUT: Diagnostic: (2203, 5)
Rhythm: (2203, 9)
Test records: 2203
CODE: diagnostic_pred = (
    diagnostic_probs
    >= DIAGNOSTIC_THRESHOLDS
).astype(int)


rhythm_pred = (
    rhythm_probs
    >= RHYTHM_THRESHOLDS

CODE: def safe_macro_auroc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):

    scores = []


    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        if len(
            np.unique(
                y_true[:, i]
            )
        ) < 2:

            continue


        scores.append(
            roc_auc_score(
                y_true[:, i],
                y_prob[:, i],
            )
        )


    if not scores:

        return float("nan")


    return float(
        np.mean(scores)
    )


def safe_macro_auprc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):

    scores = []


    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        if len(
            np.unique(
                y_true[:, i]
            )
        ) < 2:

            continue


        scores.append(
            average_precision_score(
                y_true[:, i],
                y_prob[:, i],
            )
        )


    if not scores:

        return float("nan")


    return float(
        np.mean(scores)
    )


def calculate_metrics(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    y_pred,
):

    return {

        "AUROC":
            safe_macro_auroc(
                y_true,
                y_prob,
            ),

        "AUPRC":
            safe_macro_auprc(

CODE: diagnostic_rows = []


for i, label in enumerate(
    DIAGNOSTIC_COLUMNS
):

    true = diagnostic_true[:, i]

    prob = diagnostic_probs[:, i]

    pred = diagnostic_pred[:, i]


    if len(
        np.unique(true)
    ) >= 2:

        auroc = roc_auc_score(
            true,
            prob
        )

        auprc = average_precision_score(
            true,
            prob
        )

    else:

        auroc = np.nan

        auprc = np.nan


    diagnostic_rows.append({

        "Label":
            label,

        "AUROC":
            auroc,

        "AUPRC":
            auprc,

        "F1":
            f1_score(
                true,
                pred,
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Precision":
            precision_score(
                true,
                pred,
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Recall":
            recall_score(
                true,
                pred,
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Accuracy":
            np.mean(
                true == pred
            ),

        "F1":
            f1_score(
                true,
                pred,
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Precision":
            precision_score(
                true,
                pred,
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Recall":
            recall_score(
                true,
                pred,
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Accuracy":
            np.mean(
                true == pred
            ),
    })


rhythm_report = pd.DataFrame(
    rhythm_rows
)


display(
    rhythm_report
)

CODE: SEED = 42


def set_seed(seed=42):

    random.seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)

    torch.manual_seed(seed)

    if torch.cuda.is_available():

        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)

    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False


set_seed(SEED)

CODE: class PTBXLDataset(Dataset):

    def __init__(
        self,
        dataframe,
        project_root,
        diagnostic_targets,
        rhythm_targets,
    ):

        self.df = (
            dataframe
            .reset_index(
                drop=True
            )
        )


        self.project_root = Path(
            project_root
        )


        self.diagnostic_targets = list(
            diagnostic_targets
        )


        self.rhythm_targets = list(
            rhythm_targets
        )


    def __len__(self):

        return len(
            self.df
        )


    def __getitem__(
        self,
        index,
    ):

        row = self.df.iloc[index]


        path = (
            self.project_root
            /
            row[
                "processed_path"
            ]
        )


        signal = np.load(
            path,
            allow_pickle=False,
        )


        if signal.shape != (
            SIGNAL_LENGTH,
            NUM_LEADS,
        ):

            raise ValueError(
                f"Unexpected ECG shape "
                f"{signal.shape} at {path}. "
                f"Expected "
                f"({SIGNAL_LENGTH}, "
                f"

CODE: if (
    "model_state_dict"
    in checkpoint
):

    state_dict = checkpoint[
        "model_state_dict"
    ]

elif (
    "state_dict"
    in checkpoint
):

    state_dict = checkpoint[
        "state_dict"
    ]

else:

    state_dict = checkpoint


clean_state_dict = {}


for key, value in state_dict.items():

    new_key = key


    if new_key.startswith(
        "module."
    ):

        new_key = new_key[
            len("module.") :
        ]


    clean_state_dict[
        new_key
    ] = value


missing_keys, unexpected_keys = (
    model.load_state_dict(
        clean_state_dict,
        strict=False,
    )
)


print(
    "Missing keys:",
    missing_keys
)

print(
    "Unexpected keys:",
    unexpected_keys
)


if (
    len(missing_keys) > 0
    or
    len(unexpected_keys) > 0
):

    raise RuntimeError(
        "Model/checkpoint mismatch."
    )


model.eval()

    "\nCurrent ResNet checkpoint loaded successfully."

def collect_test_predictions(
    model,
    loader,
):

    model.eval()


    diagnostic_true = []

    diagnostic_probs = []


    rhythm_true = []

    rhythm_probs = []


    for (
        signals,
        diagnostic,
        rhythm,
    ) in loader:


        signals = signals.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )


        diagnostic = diagnostic.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )


        rhythm = rhythm.to(
            DEVICE,
            non_blocking=True,
        )


        outputs = model(
            signals
        )


        diagnostic_probability = (
            torch.sigmoid(
                outputs[
                    "diagnostic"
                ]
            )
            .cpu()
            .numpy()
        )


        diagnostic_true.append(
            diagnostic
            .cpu()
            .numpy()
        )


        diagnostic_probs.append(
            diagnostic_probability
        )


        if len(
            RHYTHM_COLUMNS
        ) > 0:

            rhythm_probability = (
                torch.sigmoid(
                    outputs[
                        "rhythm"

CODE: def macro_auroc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):

    scores = []


    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        if (
            len(
                np.unique(
                    y_true[:, i]
                )
            )
            < 2
        ):

            continue


        scores.append(
            roc_auc_score(
                y_true[:, i],
                y_prob[:, i],
            )
        )


    return float(
        np.mean(scores)
    )


def macro_auprc(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
):

    scores = []


    for i in range(
        y_true.shape[1]
    ):

        scores.append(
            average_precision_score(
                y_true[:, i],
                y_prob[:, i],
            )
        )


    return float(
        np.mean(scores)
    )
CODE: def calculate_metrics(
    y_true,
    y_prob,
    y_pred,
):

    return {

        "AUROC":
            macro_auroc(
                y_true,
                y_prob,
            ),

        "AUPRC":
            macro_auprc(
                y_true,
                y_prob,
            ),

        "Macro F1":
            f1_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
                average="macro",
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Micro F1":
            f1_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
                average="micro",
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Macro Precision":
            precision_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
                average="macro",
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        "Macro Recall":
            recall_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
                average="macro",
                zero_division=0,
            ),

        # Complete label vector must match
        "Exact Match Accuracy":
            accuracy_score(
                y_true,
                y_pred,
            ),

        # Individual labels

### ml-service\notebooks\04_evaluation\ecg\04_ptbxl_model_comparison_evaluation.ipynb
# PTB-XL — CNN-BiLSTM-Attention versus CNN-Transformer comparison

This notebook compares independently trained ECG model artifacts. The original ECG evaluation notebooks remain unchanged. All comparisons must use the same PTB-XL patient-disjoint test records, labels and preprocessing version. If the recorded metadata do not prove identical splits and target columns, the comparison is flagged rather than silently accepted.
CODE: from pathlib import Path
import json, sys
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

def find_root(start=None):
    current=Path(start or Path.cwd()).resolve()
    for p in [current,*current.parents]:
        if (p/"src").is_dir() and (p/"config").is_dir(): return p
        if (p/"ml-service"/"src").is_dir(): return p/"ml-service"
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not locate ml-service.")

}
## 1. Load model-level metrics and verify provenance
CODE: records=[]
for model_name,path in MODEL_FILES.items():
    if not path.is_file():
        print(f"[MISSING] {model_name}: {path}")
        continue
    payload=json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    records.append({"model":model_name,"path":str(path),"payload":payload})
if len(records)<2:
    print("Both model artifacts are required for a direct comparative table. Train/evaluate the missing model first.")
## 2. Compare only metrics with matching definitions

The legacy CNN-BiLSTM notebook stores one aggregate metric dictionary per task group. The CNN-Transformer notebook stores metrics per label. This cell reports both faithfully: legacy aggregate values remain labelled `reported_aggregate`, while Transformer per-label values and their unweighted macro means remain explicitly labelled `per_label` and `macro_mean`. These are not automatically interchangeable. Do not claim a direct numerical winner unless the two experiments are recomputed with the same patient split, label masks, threshold policy and aggregation definition.
CODE: summary_rows=[]
label_rows=[]
for item in records:
    payload=item["payload"]
    if item["model"]=="CNN-BiLSTM-Attention":
        groups={"diagnostic":payload.get("diagnostic_test",{}),"rhythm":payload.get("rhythm_test",{})}
        for group_name,values in groups.items():
            if isinstance(values,dict) and values and all(not isinstance(v,dict) for v in values.values()):
                summary_rows.append({"model":item["model"],"group":group_name,"aggregation":"reported_aggregate",**values})
            elif isinstance(values,dict):
                for label,metric in values.items():
                    if isinstance(metric,dict): label_rows.append({"model":item["model"],"group":group_name,"label":label,**metric})
    else:
        groups=payload.get("test",{})
        for group_name,values in groups.items():
            if not isinstance(values,dict): continue
            current=[]
            for label,metric in values.items():
                if not isinstance(metric,dict): continue
                row={"model":item["model"],"group":group_name,"label":label,**metric}
                label_rows.append(row); current.append(metric)

OUTPUT: Task-group summary (aggregation method is shown explicitly):
OUTPUT: Per-label rows (available for models whose artifacts save them):

## 3. Fair-comparison checklist

Before claiming one model is better, verify:

1. The same manifest version and patient-level train/validation/test partition were used.
2. The same diagnostic and rhythm target columns were evaluated.
3. Metrics are calculated on the same held-out patient records.
4. The same threshold-selection procedure was used, with thresholds selected on validation data only.
5. AUROC, AUPRC, sensitivity, specificity, F1 and calibration are reported per label.
6. Confidence intervals are calculated using patient-level bootstrap resampling if multiple ECG records per patient are present.

If these conditions are not verified, this notebook provides an artifact inventory, not a valid head-to-head performance claim.

    path = Path(value)
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = PROJECT_ROOT / path
    return path.resolve()

)

if isinstance(checkpoint, dict):
    if "model_state_dict" in checkpoint:

    elif "state_dict" in checkpoint:
        state_dict = checkpoint["state_dict"]

    else:
        state_dict = checkpoint

        new_key = new_key[len("module."):]

    clean_state_dict,

)

### ml-service\notebooks\04_evaluation\echo\03_panecho_finetuning_evaluation.ipynb

CODE: def calibrate_binary_target(
    dataframe,
    true_column,
    probability_column,
    output_path,
):
    y_true = (
        dataframe[true_column]
        .to_numpy()
    )

    raw_prob = (
        dataframe[probability_column]
        .to_numpy()
    )

    calibrator = PlattCalibrator()

    calibrator.fit(
        raw_prob,
        y_true,
    )

    calibrated_prob = (
        calibrator.predict_proba(
            raw_prob
        )
    )

    metrics = calibration_metrics(
        y_true,
        raw_prob,
        calibrated_prob,
    )

    calibrator.save(
        output_path
    )

    return (
        calibrator,
        calibrated_prob,
        metrics,
    )

CODE: def ef_to_raw_probability(
    predicted_ef,
    threshold,
    scale=5.0,
):
    predicted_ef = np.asarray(
        predicted_ef,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    # Lower EF -> higher probability.
    score = (
        threshold
        - predicted_ef
    ) / scale

    return 1.0 / (
        1.0 + np.exp(-score)
    )

### ml-service\notebooks\05_calibration_fusion\02_experiment_calibration_validation.ipynb
# CardioFusion-XAI — calibration fitting and validation

This notebook is the executable calibration workflow. It uses held-out **validation** predictions only. It must never use test predictions to fit calibrators or prediction intervals.

- Binary classification endpoints: fit source/target-specific Platt calibrators when there are enough validation examples from both classes.
- Multiclass outputs: remain explicitly uncalibrated unless a separately validated multiclass calibration method is implemented.
- Echo EF: calculate a split-conformal absolute-residual interval from validation predictions only.
- Different dataset endpoints are never pooled into one calibrator.
CODE: from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.metrics import brier_score_loss, log_loss

def find_root(start=None):
    current=Path(start or Path.cwd()).resolve()
    for p in [current,*current.parents]:
        if (p/"src").is_dir() and (p/"config").is_dir(): return p
        if (p/"ml-service"/"src").is_dir(): return p/"ml-service"
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not locate ml-service.")

CODE: def fit_binary_groups(frame, group_columns, probability_col="prediction", truth_col="true", output_subdir=None):
    required=set(group_columns)|{probability_col,truth_col}
    missing=required-set(frame.columns)
    if missing: raise ValueError(f"Prediction file missing columns: {sorted(missing)}")
    results=[]
    for key,group in frame.groupby(group_columns):
        if not isinstance(key,tuple): key=(key,)
        p=pd.to_numeric(group[probability_col],errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
        y=pd.to_numeric(group[truth_col],errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
        valid=np.isfinite(p)&np.isfinite(y); p=p[valid]; y=y[valid]
        name="/".join(map(str,key))
        if len(p)<10 or set(np.unique(y))!={0.0,1.0}:
            results.append({"group":name,"status":"not_fitted","n":len(p),"reason":"need >=10 finite validation records and both binary classes"})
            continue
        calibrator=PlattCalibrator().fit(p,y)
        calibrated=calibrator.predict_proba(p)
        metric=calibration_metrics(y,p,calibrated)

## 2. Fit EF split-conformal absolute-error interval

The quantile is estimated using validation residuals. This produces a marginal prediction interval under exchangeability assumptions; it is not a patient-specific epistemic uncertainty estimate and should be assessed for coverage on a separate test cohort.
CODE: echo_candidates=[
    ML_ROOT/"artifacts"/"metrics"/"echo"/"panecho"/"finetuned"/"validation_predictions.csv",
    ML_ROOT/"artifacts"/"metrics"/"echo"/"panecho"/"finetuned"/"predictions_validation.csv",
]

if echo_path is None:
    print("Echo validation prediction file not found. Run the existing PanEcho fine-tuning/evaluation notebooks first.")
else:
    echo=pd.read_csv(echo_path)
    pred_col=next((c for c in ["EF_pred","ef_pred","prediction"] if c in echo.columns),None)
    true_col=next((c for c in ["EF_true","ef_true","EF","ef"] if c in echo.columns),None)
    if pred_col is None or true_col is None: raise ValueError(f"Cannot identify EF prediction/truth columns in {echo_path}; columns={list(echo.columns)}")
    pred=pd.to_numeric(echo[pred_col],errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
    true=pd.to_numeric(echo[true_col],errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
    valid=np.isfinite(pred)&np.isfinite(true)
    residuals=np.abs(true[valid]-pred[valid])
    if len(residuals)<20: raise ValueError("At least 20 finite validation EF residuals are required for this interval estimate.")

## 3. Calibration audit and safeguards

Review that all input CSVs contain validation predictions only. Calibration is performed per endpoint and per source, not across dataset populations. A successfully fitted calibrator does not make a source endpoint clinically generalizable; it only adjusts probabilities for the source-defined target under the calibration population assumptions.

### ml-service\notebooks\05_calibration_fusion\03_selected_models_calibration_fusion.ipynb
# Calibration of the selected models
Run the calibration-ready biomarker, ECG and PanEcho notebooks first. This workflow uses the selected GBDT biomarker experts, CNN-BiLSTM-Attention, and PanEcho only. Platt scaling is fit on reserved calibration predictions and evaluated on untouched test predictions. Under-supported targets remain uncalibrated. EF uses split conformal absolute residuals; the resulting interval has marginal coverage under exchangeability, not patient-specific epistemic uncertainty.
## 1. Paths, calibration utilities and artifact checks

def file_sha256(path):
    digest=hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path,'rb') as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda:stream.read(1024*1024),b''):digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()

    d=pd.read_csv(path);needed={'record_id','target','y_true','y_prob

CODE: def _scalarize(value):
    if isinstance(value, list):
        if not value:
            return None
        return float(pd.Series(value).mean())
    return value

    modality: {
        target: _scalarize(value)
        for target, value in predictions.items()
        if value is not None

}

### ml-service\notebooks\05_calibration_fusion\05_staged_inference_fusion.ipynb
# Staged Inference

This notebook runs inference through the CardioFusion-XAI staged fusion pipeline:

`Biomarkers → ECG → Echo`

The notebook:
- loads the disease registry,
- loads calibrated modality models,
- accepts whichever modalities are available,
- produces disease-specific risk and confidence,
- recommends missing/confirmatory modalities,
- does not blindly average or multiply probabilities,
- keeps unsupported diseases disabled.

CODE: # Cell 4 — Prepare inference inputs
#
# Replace these example dictionaries with outputs produced by your
# actual trained modality models.

# Biomarker predictions:
biomarker_outputs = {}

# ECG predictions:
ecg_outputs = {}

# Echo prediction:
echo_outputs = {}

# Example structure:
#
# biomarker_outputs = {
#     "acute_mi": 0.91,
#     "hf_mortality": 0.28,
#     "ten_year_chd_risk": 0.41,
# }
#
# ecg_outputs = {
#     "acute_mi": 0.73,
#     "atrial_fibrillation": 0.88,
#     "av_block": 0.12,
# }
#
# echo_outputs = {
#     "ef": 32.5,
#     "reduced_lvef": 0.93,
#     "reduced_ef_cardiomyopathy": 0.82,
# }

modality_outputs = {
    "biomarkers": biomarker_outputs,
    "ecg": ecg_outputs,
    "echo": echo_outputs,
}

for modality, outputs in modality_outputs.items():
    print(f"{modality}: {len(outputs)} outputs")
OUTPUT: Available modality evidence:
biomarkers: 0 outputs
ecg: 0 outputs
echo: 0 outputs
CODE: # Cell 5 — Basic input validation

def validate_probability(value, name):
    value = float(value)
    if not 0.0 <= value <= 1.0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be between 0 and 1, got {value}")
    return value

def validate_modality_outputs(outputs):
    if not isinstance(outputs, dict):
        raise TypeError("Each modality output must be a dictionary.")

    cleaned = {}

    for key, value in outputs.items():
        if value is None:
            continue

        # Echo EF is a measurement, not a probability.
        if key.lower() in {"ef", "ejection_fraction", "lvef"}:
            cleaned[key] = float(value)
        else:
            cleaned[key] = validate_probability(value, f"{key}")

    return cleaned

modality_outputs = {
    modality: validate_modality_outputs(outputs)
    for modality, outputs in modality_outputs.items()
}

print("Input validation complete.")
OUTPUT: Input validation complete.
CODE: # Cell 7 - Run staged inference
# Inputs remain empty until real patient modality predictions are supplied.
# The engine still evaluates every registered disease and reports missing evidence.
fusion_engine = StagedFusionEngine(
    registry=registry,
)

results = fusion_engine.evaluate_all(modality_outputs)

results_list = list(results.values()) if isinstance(results, dict) else list(results)
results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_list)

print(f"Generated {len(results_list)} disease results.")
OUTPUT: Generated 26 disease results.
CODE: # Cell 7 — Run staged inference
#
# The engine follows the registry:
# - unsupported diseases remain unsupported,
# - missing primary modality -> risk=None + recommendation,
# - available primary -> calibrated primary risk,
# - confirmatory agreement -> confidence upgrade,
# - disagreement -> confidence capped at MODERATE,
# - no blind probability averaging/multiplication.

results = fusion_engine.evaluate_all(modality_outputs)

if isinstance(results, dict):
    results_list = list(results.values())
else:
    results_list = list(results)

results_df = pd.DataFrame(results_list)

print(f"Generated {len(results_list)} disease results.")
OUTPUT: Generated 26 disease results.

CODE: # Cell 10 — Human-readable staged report

def format_risk(risk):
    if risk is None or pd.isna(risk):
        return "Not available"
    return f"{float(risk) * 100:.1f}%"

print("=" * 80)
print("CARDIOFUSION-XAI — STAGED INFERENCE REPORT")
print("=" * 80)

for _, row in results_df.iterrows():
    print(f"\n{row['display_name']}")

    print(f"  Status      : {row['status']}")
    print(f"  Risk        : {format_risk(row['risk'])}")
    print(f"  Confidence  : {row['confidence']}")
    print(f"  Detected by : {row.get('detected_from', [])}")

    recommendations = row.get("recommendations", [])
    if isinstance(recommendations, str):
        recommendations = [recommendations]

    for recommendation in recommendations:
        print(f"  Recommendation: {recommendation}")

The orchestrator does not calculate a combined disease probability from unpaired datasets. It preserves each source-specific result and produces an evidence completeness / recommendation report.

## 3. Inspect endpoint-level requirements and missing-evidence recommendations

This is a configuration inspection. It ensures endpoints have an explicit target/source and output semantics. It does not manufacture model predictions for unuploaded modalities.
CODE: # Registry data is shown as loaded from YAML so its actual schema remains the source of truth.
if isinstance(registry,dict):
    rows=[]
    for name,spec in registry.items():
        if not isinstance(spec,dict): continue
        rows.append({"endpoint":name,"primary_modality":spec.get("primary_modality"),
                     "prediction_sources":str(spec.get("prediction_sources",spec.get("sources",[]))),
                     "output_type":str(spec.get("output",spec.get("probability_policy",{}))),

## 4. Software invariants to enforce in API / unit tests

- An unavailable modality must be `not_assessed`, never probability 0.
- An uploaded-but-failed modality must be `processing_failed` or `unsupported_input`, not `not_uploaded`.
- A continuous EF measurement must never be rendered as a percentage probability.
- UCI-HF mortality requires an existing-HF population context.
- MI-complications outcomes require prior MI context.
- PTB-XL annotation probabilities and Zheen heart-attack classification probabilities are different endpoints; no arithmetic comparison is allowed.
- No global agreement score can be interpreted as clinical confidence.
- Combined disease probability is disabled in this unpaired-data setting.
CODE: # Static contract audit of the registry's unpaired-data policy.
policy_text=registry_text
assert "do_not_average" in policy_text or "numeric_fusion" in policy_text or "combined_probability" in policy_text
print("Evidence orchestration policy contract inspected. Use tests/test_fusion.py and tests/test_api.py for executable contract tests.")
OUTPUT: Evidence orchestration policy contract inspected. Use tests/test_fusion.py and tests/test_api.py for executable contract tests.

### ml-service\notebooks\06_explainability\02_ecg_signal_explainability.ipynb

## 2. Select an output and calculate Integrated Gradients

The output is selected by the highest inference probability (calibrated where a matching calibration artifact exists). Integrated Gradients attributes the selected model head's logit relative to a zero-waveform baseline; the attribution is not a gradient of calibrated probability. This is a demonstration, not a thresholded diagnosis. For a research experiment, pre-register the label of interest and aggregate explanations over an independent test cohort.
CODE: from src.xai.ecg_explainer import integrated_gradients_ecg

scores = models.predict_ecg(waveform)
# predict_ecg returns scalar probabilities keyed by output label, plus *_raw and _metadata entries.
target_names = [*models.ecg_diagnostic_targets, *models.ecg_rhythm_targets]
target_scores = {
    name.removeprefix("RHYTHM_"): float(scores[name.removeprefix("RHYTHM_")])
    for name in target_names
    if name.removeprefix("RHYTHM_") in scores
}
if not target_scores:
    raise RuntimeError("The ECG model returned no scores matching its target manifest.")
if not np.isfinite(list(target_scores.values())).all():
    raise ValueError("The ECG model returned a non-finite target probability.")
ranked = sorted(target_scores.items(), key=lambda item: item[1], reverse=True)
label, probability = ranked[0]
lookup = {name: ("diagnostic", index) for index, name in enumerate(models.ecg_diagnostic_targets)}
lookup.update({name.removeprefix("RHYTHM_"): ("rhythm", index) for index, name in enumerate(models.ecg_rhythm_targets)})
if label not in lookup:
    raise KeyError(f"Selected target {label!r} is not in the checkpoint target manifest.")
task, target_index = lookup[label]
explanation = integrated_g

Method: IntegratedGradients

## 4. Interpretation limits

- The baseline is a zero waveform; alternative baselines should be evaluated for sensitivity.
- Attribution magnitude indicates model sensitivity relative to the chosen baseline.
- It does not show the causal contribution of an ECG feature or localize an anatomical lesion.
- Report explanation stability across several records and seeds, not only one selected trace.
- PTB-XL labels are dataset annotations; they are not a replacement for a clinician's ECG interpretation.

### ml-service\notebooks\06_explainability\03_echo_video_explainability.ipynb
# CardioFusion-XAI — EchoNet-Dynamic temporal explainability

This notebook uses the trained PanEcho model and a real EchoNet-Dynamic test video to perform temporal-window occlusion. It measures how the EF output changes when consecutive video-frame windows are replaced by a median-frame baseline.

This is a perturbation explanation, not SHAP, not anatomical localization and not a causal explanation. It does not turn EF into a disease probability.
CODE: from pathlib import Path
import os
import sys


def find_ml_service_root(start=None):
    current = Path(start or Path.cwd()).resolve()
    for candidate in [current, *current.parents]:
        if (candidate / "pyproject.toml").is_file() and (candidate / "src").is_dir() and (candidate / "config").is_dir():
            return candidate
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        "Could not locate ml-service/. Start Jupyter from the repository or from ml-service/."
    )


ML_ROOT = find_ml_service_root()
os.chdir(ML_ROOT)
if str(ML_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ML_ROOT))
print(f"ML project root: {ML_ROOT}")

## 3. Perform temporal occlusion

The prediction function accepts the same `[number_of_clips, 3, 16, 224, 224]` tensor shape as the production PanEcho inference method. The explainer converts Torch tensors safely to CPU arrays before perturbation.
CODE: import torch
from src.models.echo.panecho import decode_outputs


def predict_ef_from_clips(array: np.ndarray) -> float:
    tensor = torch.as_tensor(array, dtype=torch.float32, device=models.device)
    with torch.inference_mode():
        outputs = models.echo_model.predict_clips(tensor)
        decoded = decode_outputs(outputs)
    return float(decoded["EF"]["value"])

clips_np = clips.detach().cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32)
explanation = temporal_occlusion_explanation(predict_ef_from_clips, clips_np, window_frames=4)
print("Baseline EF:", explanation["original_output"])
print("Explanation:", explanation["method"])
OUTPUT: Baseline EF: 57.28301239013672
Explanation: temporal_window_occlusion
## 4. Plot the temporal contribution pattern
CODE: import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

windows = explanation["windows"]
starts = [item["start_frame"] for item in windows]
differences = [item["output_difference"] for item in windows]
fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(9, 4))
ax.bar(starts, differences, width=3.5)
ax.set_title("PanEcho EF change under temporal-window occlusion")
ax.set_xlabel("Start frame (within selected clip tensor)")
ax.set_ylabel("Original EF − occluded EF (percentage points)")
ax.grid(axis="y", alpha=0.25)
plt.show()
## 5. Interpretation limits

- Occlusion scores depend on the chosen median-frame baseline and window size.
- A large output difference does not establish a causal anatomical region.
- Report stability across multiple test videos and multiple window sizes.
- EF remains a continuous measurement; it must not be rendered as a disease probability.
- Use clinically reviewed heatmaps and other methods as complementary evidence, not as a substitute for image interpretation.
